# Agents · D · part 5

888 entries, deploy-risk-scanner to design-reviewer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **deploy-risk-scanner**: Scans a git diff for deployment risks — database migrations, env var additions, background job schema changes, webhook/API contract changes. Returns a risk level and categorized list of findings. Invoked during the build/push review step alongside the four review agents; a HIGH risk level is treated as a BLOCKER. ([joshevensen/hexbyte-plugins](https://github.com/joshevensen/hexbyte-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/orc/agents/deploy-risk-scanner.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **deploy-runbook**: &gt; Deploy canônico = GitHub Actions (D039/D041). VM manual só bootstrap/diagnóstico/rollback aprovado. &gt; Blindagens da esteira: spec 009. Cicatrizes: D041 (nunca `down` por prefixo global), flock, snapshot, rollback. &gt; &gt; **Esteira consolidada (spec 026 F4/F5):** os antigos `deploy-{mesas,glossario,site,accounts}.yml` &gt; foram REMOVIDOS e substituídos por **um único `deploy.yml` declarativo** que lê ([FarenRavirar/artificio](https://github.com/FarenRavirar/artificio/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/deploy-runbook.md))
- **deploy-runner**: Deploys a single package to the dev environment using CDK via aws-vault. ([Josh-S-N2M/feature-pipeline](https://github.com/Josh-S-N2M/feature-pipeline/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/design-frontend.md))
- **design-gap-analyzer**: Detects design-implementation gaps using Serena MCP for symbol-level analysis. Self-sufficient agent that autonomously gathers and analyzes data. ([chkim-su/forge-editor](https://github.com/chkim-su/forge-editor/blob/HEAD/agents/design-gap-analyzer.md) · ★1)
- **design-gatekeeper**: Use when a main session is about to start any logo, icon, brand-mark, or visual-identity work and you need to enforce the design-engine contract — medium detection, font/token scan, reference measurement, acceptance-criteria approval, design push gate, and 3-strike escalation. Blocks downstream actions that would bypass the gates and prevents the "logo session death spiral" failure mode (50+ iterations on a 5-minute hex change). ([infra-ecomma/devin-hackathon](https://github.com/infra-ecomma/devin-hackathon/blob/HEAD/packages/tellurion/.claude/agents/design-gatekeeper.md))
- **design-gauntlet**: Post-synthesis design quality gate. Evaluates the implemented design on user-task clarity, hierarchy, distinctiveness, content authenticity, interaction quality, responsive integrity, and reference adaptation when applicable. Runs after the critic ensemble and critique-synthesizer. It does not replace critics or the accessibility auditor. ([raymondware/kern](https://github.com/raymondware/kern/blob/HEAD/agents/design-gauntlet.md) · ★1)
- **design-generator**: UI implementation specialist that creates production-ready HTML, CSS, and design tokens. ([Claude-Code-Community-Ireland/claude-code-resources](https://github.com/Claude-Code-Community-Ireland/claude-code-resources/blob/HEAD/plugins/vibeworks-library/agents/design-generator.md) · ★10)
- **design-generator**: Use after design-architect locks the brief. Generates 3 implementation variants using 21st.dev Magic MCP (2 variants) and Vercel v0 MCP (1 variant). Refactors raw MCP output into @arcanea/design-system tokens before presenting to user. Never ships raw hex, never accepts generic patterns. ([frankxai/arcanea-ai-app](https://github.com/frankxai/arcanea-ai-app/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/design-generator.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **design-generator**: claude.ai/design-quality HTML/CSS generator. Takes a brief and produces ec-* namespaced, oklch() colored, self-contained design artifacts. - **design-grader**: Grade a built surface against a design reference (spec / figma / screenshot). - **design-grill**: 발행(git push) 직전에 호출되어, dod-checker(규칙 기계 검출)가 못 잡는 "판단" 차원의 질문을 던지는 편집자 역할. 아직 push 안 된 변경(origin/main..HEAD + 미커밋 + untracked)을 .claude/rules/ 컨텍스트로 읽고 구체적(파일:라인, 문장 인용) Socratic 질문을 생성한다. 사실성과 익명화를 최우선으로 캐묻는다. 자동 수정·점수화 금지, 변경 없는 축은 섹션 생략. 답변은 docs/decisions/ ADR로 박제된다(grill-with-docs).
